Then 96:9 and 96:11 repeated `أرأيت`, breaking the closed mirror of `رآه`; 96:12 supplied protected self-guarding as an alternative to imagined self-sufficiency. The later counter-gaze was the main surprise and confirmed that the error lies in self-assessment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001110/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1f79a2815af94fbe2afc","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:7:3\",\"root_001110\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Ses ve ezgi çağrışımı, yeterlilik yanılgısının kişinin kendi söyleyişini denetlemesinden doğan gösterişli güveni de kapsayabileceğini düşündürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001110/B001","root_001110/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_260d23bd6d0acf1eeabc","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:7:3\",\"root_001110\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kendini yeterli görmek, insanın kökenini ve öğretimini taşıyan kaynakların yerine kendisini koyma girişimidir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2a447660bd600e89bcde","text":"The later repeated `أرأيت` surprised me by turning “he saw himself” into a contested seeing-chain. Human self-seeing is answered by interrogative seeing and finally divine seeing in 96:14.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8071495daa25f37a0f4c","text":"3. **A mirror-image challenged by a counter-gaze.** `ر ء ي:B006` supplies visible appearance and mirror, and `ر ء ي:B013` supplies the alerting “tell me” formula. Reflexive `رآه` is followed by two `أرأيت` prompts inside the window (96:9, 96:11). The mirror branch makes the first gaze self-enclosed; the later formulae break that enclosure by making another observer judge the conduct. I infer that the repeated root deliberately changes the direction of scrutiny. The ayah reads as self-image mistaken for reality until a counter-gaze is summoned.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001110/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_869f732396fb4a8fa6e1","text":"2. **Self-sufficiency as attempted substitution.** `غ ن ي:B002` includes sufficing, availing, or standing in for another. The human originates in attachment (96:2), receives teaching (96:4–5), and is immediately told that return is to the Rabb (96:8). The replacement branch’s role is to expose what the reflexive judgment attempts: the self is treated as if it could stand in for every sustaining source. The packet gives dependence and return; I infer the attempted substitution. `استغنى` thus reads as a claim of functional replacement, not only possession of abundance.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B001","root_001110/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_886534c774e3c7436e9b","text":"1. Self-sufficiency is triggered by self-seeing, so the problem is perception before possession. Evidence: `ر ء ي B001` seeing/insight, `ر ء ي B002` reflective judgment/opinion, `غ ن ي B001` wealth/self-sufficiency, `غ ن ي B002` sufficing/availing. Structural cue: the cause is `أن رآه`, “that he saw himself,” not that he actually became independent. Mechanism: perception produces a judgment of sufficiency; the inner opinion substitutes for the Lord's teaching and creating. Abductive move: the packet gives seeing and self-sufficiency; I infer that the causal arrow is psychological: seeing-as creates rebellion. Reading change: the ayah reads as diagnosis of self-misperception, not merely condemnation of wealth. 2. `استغنى` is reversed by the origin from `علق` and by the return in 96:8. Evidence: `ع ل ق B006/B009` meager dependence/conception taking hold, `ر ج ع B001` return, `غ ن ي B001/B002` sufficiency. Structural cue: 96:2 and 96:8 enclose 96:7: from clinging origin to return to the Lord. Mechanism: the claim of independence is trapped between dependence at origin and return at end. Abductive move: the packet supplies the sequence; I infer that self-sufficiency is a temporary optical illusion inside a return-arc. Reading change: `استغنى` reads as a false interruption of dependence, not as a stable state. 3. The voice/sound branch of wealth is weak but activated by the recitation setting. Evidence: `غ ن ي B003` song/vocal modulation, `ق ر ء B002` recitation, `ر ج ع B007` modulated/repeated sound. Structural cue: the passage begins with repeated `ٱقْرَأْ`, and later contains repeated `أرأيت`. Mechanism: the human may mistake control of voiced performance for self-sufficiency. Abductive move: the packet gives vocal branches but no explicit singing; I infer only an exploratory secondary reading through the sonic repetition of the packet. Reading change: self-sufficiency can include performative confidence in one's own voice, not only material independence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B006","root_000531/B013"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b01920780ec783ea08a7","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:7:2\",\"root_000531\",[\"B006\",\"B013\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kişinin kendi görüntüsüne kapanan bakışı, sonraki 'gördün mü' soruları ve Allah'ın görmesiyle dışarıdan bir karşı bakışa açılır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c0017ab00f2f4792115c","text":"1. **A causal loop driven by judgment, not verified independence.** `ر ء ي:B002` supplies opinion and reflective judgment, while `غ ن ي:B001` supplies wealth or lack of need. The reflexive object in `رآه` makes the human both assessor and assessed; repeated `أرأيت` in 96:9 and 96:11 then introduces an external counter-assessment. The packet supplies self-seeing as the stated trigger; I infer that the decisive event is a judgment of sufficiency, not sufficiency independently established. The line therefore diagnoses transgression as an epistemic feedback loop.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B002","root_001110/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_d4dfb0c6dfb2b838bba5","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:7:2\",\"root_000531\",[\"B002\"]],[\"96:7:3\",\"root_001110\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Taşkınlığı doğuran şey gerçek bağımsızlık değil, kişinin hem yargıç hem yargılanan olduğu kapalı bir öz değerlendirmeyle kendisini yeterli saymasıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
